---
title: "Schemas"
description: "The 476 shared shapes referenced by the operation reference."
---

{/* Generated by scripts/openapi/generate-docs-reference.ts. Do not edit; run npm run docs:reference. */}

Named shapes that operations reference with `$ref: "#/components/schemas/<Name>"`. Each one is converted from the zod schema or TypeScript type its route handler uses.

## AcceptedRef

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "ref": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "label": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "type": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "entity",
        "setting",
        "textroot",
        "packet"
      ]
    },
    "lifecycleState": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "render_provisional",
        "commit_validated"
      ]
    },
    "provenance": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "href": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "ref",
    "label",
    "type",
    "lifecycleState",
    "provenance",
    "href"
  ]
}
```

## AesErrorEnvelope

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "ok",
    "error"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "ok": {
      "const": false
    },
    "error": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "code",
        "message"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "code": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "message": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "retryable": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "correlationId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "retryAfterMs": {
          "type": "number",
          "description": "Present on some retryable errors; on 409 and 429 responses, Retry-After carries the same delay in seconds."
        },
        "details": {
          "type": "object",
          "description": "Authorization denials carry reason (the denial, e.g. missing_scope or world_policy_denied) and sometimes detail."
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

## AgentWorkflow

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "workflowId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "summary": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "keywords": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "description": "Words an agent may search for to find this workflow."
    },
    "credential": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowCredential",
      "description": "The credential the steps send."
    },
    "parameters": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "description": "Path-template parameters and where their values come from."
    },
    "rules": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "description": "How to run the ordered steps."
    },
    "contractRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "steps": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowStep"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "contractRef",
    "keywords",
    "parameters",
    "rules",
    "steps",
    "summary",
    "title",
    "workflowId"
  ],
  "description": "AES §8.10.7 — an ordered, machine-readable recipe over registry operations."
}
```

## AgentWorkflowCondition

```json
{
  "anyOf": [
    {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "field": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "in": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "number",
              "boolean"
            ]
          }
        },
        "step": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "field",
        "in"
      ]
    },
    {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "field": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "notIn": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "number",
              "boolean"
            ]
          }
        },
        "step": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "field",
        "notIn"
      ]
    },
    {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "field": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "contains": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "step": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "field",
        "contains"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "description": "AES §8.10.7 — a condition an agent evaluates against a JSON response. `field` is a dot path into the response body; a `[]` segment maps over an array (`workflow.nextActions[].action`). The pseudo-field `httpStatus` is the response's HTTP status. `step` names an earlier step whose latest response is read instead of the current context."
}
```

## AgentWorkflowCredential

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "kind": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "api_key",
        "room_link",
        "room_link_and_api_key"
      ]
    },
    "send": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "The header form, e.g. `Authorization: Bearer <inviteToken>`."
    },
    "rule": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "What the credential acts as, and what sending the other credential beside it does."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "kind",
    "send",
    "rule"
  ],
  "description": "The credential every step of a workflow sends, and what it acts as. `room_link_and_api_key`: a room-link private lane, where the invite token names the seat and the API key supplies its owner's authority, both on every call."
}
```

## AgentWorkflowStep

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "stepId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "routeId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "method": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "GET",
        "POST",
        "PUT",
        "PATCH",
        "DELETE"
      ]
    },
    "pathTemplate": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "action": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "The submission `workflow.nextActions[].action` id this step answers, when there is one."
    },
    "purpose": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "optional": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "True when some runs of the workflow skip this step."
    },
    "when": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowCondition"
      },
      "description": "The step applies only when every condition holds against the latest resource read. Absent: always."
    },
    "body": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "required": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        },
        "optional": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "required",
        "optional"
      ]
    },
    "query": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "required": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        },
        "optional": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "required",
        "optional"
      ],
      "description": "Query-string fields, under the same rules as `body`."
    },
    "headers": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "required": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        },
        "optional": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "required",
        "optional"
      ],
      "description": "Request headers other than the workflow credential, under the same rules as `body`."
    },
    "advanceWhen": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowCondition"
      },
      "description": "The step is done when every condition holds against this step's own response."
    },
    "pollIntervalMs": {
      "type": "number",
      "description": "Polling steps repeat the call at this interval until `advanceWhen` holds."
    },
    "notes": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "stepId",
    "routeId",
    "method",
    "pathTemplate",
    "purpose",
    "optional",
    "advanceWhen"
  ],
  "description": "One call in a workflow. `routeId` is the OpenAPI `operationId`: a `ROUTE_AUTH_POLICIES` routeId or a chat operation binding in `SHIPPED_ROUTE_COVERAGE`; `method` and `pathTemplate` are copied from it."
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
      "type": "array",

      },
```

```json
      "properties": {
        "required": {

          "items": {

          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "required",
        "optional"
      ]
    },
    "query": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "required": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        },
        "optional": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "required",
        "optional"
      ],
      "description": "Query-string fields, under the same rules as `body`."
    },
    "headers": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "required": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        },
        "optional": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "required",
        "optional"
      ],
      "description": "Request headers other than the workflow credential, under the same rules as `body`."
    },
    "advanceWhen": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowCondition"
      },
      "description": "The step is done when every condition holds against this step's own response."
    },

      "type": "number",

    },
    "notes": {
      "type": "string"
    },
```

```json
{
  "type": "object",
```

```json
    },
    "controlTagPresent": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "closeTagPresent": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "parsed": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "schemaValid": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "suppressedByTerminalGuard": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "terminalReason": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "empty_output",
        "boilerplate",
        "greeting_off_contract",
        "thin_substrate"
      ]
    },
    "sourceDraftStatus": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "accepted",
        "rejected",
        "fallback_synthesized",
        "absent"
      ],
      "description": "The report status that the room owner will persist for the final turn report if it trusts this verdict as-is. `draft.verdict_absent` uses the reserved `'absent'` marker until the room owner resolves the fallback."
    },
    "visibleTextLength": {
      "type": "number"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "kind",
    "schemaVersion",
    "code",
    "cognitionSupplied",
    "instructionInPrompt",
    "controlTagPresent",
    "closeTagPresent",

    "visibleTextLength"
  ]
}
```

## ChatFeedLiveEntry

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "eventKind": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatFeedLiveEventKind"
    },
    "eventId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "sessionId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "occurredAt": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "actorRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "participantRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "admittedWorldId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "importedVoiceId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "introductionRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "surfacedParticipantRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "messageId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "turnId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "sceneRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "activeHandleRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "linkedSessionRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "executionStatus": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "active",
        "suspended",
        "closed"
      ]
    },
    "turnsCompleted": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "sceneStatus": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "active",
        "completed",
        "cancelled",
        "timed_out"
      ]
    },
    "closeReason": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "stream": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "sequence": {
          "type": "number",
          "description": "Order of this event within its turn, starting at 1."
        },
        "sourceMessageId": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "The room message the turn answers."
        },
        "frame": {
          "type": "object",
          "additionalProperties": {},
          "description": "`persona.stream.delta`: the frame exactly as `/continue` streams it."
        },
        "status": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatFeedStreamStatus",
          "description": "`persona.stream.completed`: how the turn's stream ended."
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "sequence"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "eventId",
    "eventKind",
    "occurredAt",
    "sessionId",
    "stream",
    "turnId"
  ],
  "description": "In-flight room feed event (HAM_CHAT §4.6a). While a room reply is generated, `turn.started`, `persona.stream.delta`, and `persona.stream.completed` ride the room feed stream. They are not durable feed truth: `getFeed` never returns them, they carry no resume id, and `turn.snapshot.completed` remains the reply's commit boundary."
}
```

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "status": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "pending_acceptance",
        "active"
      ]
    },
    "seat": {
      "type": "object",

          "type": [
            "string",
            "null"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "participantRef"
      ],
      "description": "The room participant every authorizedSurfaces call acts as."
    },
    "authorization": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "transport": {
          "type": "string",
          "const": "authorization_bearer"
        },
        "headerTemplate": {
          "type": "string",
          "const": "Authorization: Bearer <inviteToken>"
        },
        "tokenSource": {
          "type": "string",
          "const": "inviteToken_path_parameter"
        },
        "rules": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "description": "Which credential each kind of operation takes, and what mixing them does."
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "transport",
        "headerTemplate",
        "tokenSource",
        "rules"
      ]
    },
    "authorizedSurfaces": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "roomHead": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "feed": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "history": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "callableState": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "search": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "presence": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "heartbeat": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "messages": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        },
        "continue": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatRoomLinkSurface"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "roomHead",
        "feed",
        "history",
        "callableState",
        "search",
        "presence",
        "heartbeat"
      ],
      "description": "messages and continue appear only when the grant allows posting."
    },
    "privateLanes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ChatPrivateLaneDescriptor"
      }
    },
    "huelvaUsage": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "privateLaneRef": {
          "type": "string",
          "const": "huelva"
        },
        "addressExamples": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "privateLaneRef",
        "addressExamples"
      ],
      "description": "Legacy alias generated from privateLanes."
    },
    "discovery": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "publicDocument": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "method": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "GET"
            },
            "path": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "/.well-known/ham"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "method",
            "path"
          ]
        },
        "openapi": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "method": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "GET"
            },
            "path": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "/api/v1/openapi.json"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "method",
            "path"
          ]
        },
        "callerDirectory": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "method": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "GET"
            },
            "path": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "/api/v1/account"
            },
            "credential": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "X-Api-Key: <apiKey>"
            },
            "resultField": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "data.directory"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "method",
            "path",
            "credential",
            "resultField"
          ]
        },
        "workflows": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "method": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "GET"
            },
            "path": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "/.well-known/ham"
            },
            "resultField": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "workflows"
            },
            "workflowIds": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "method",
            "path",
            "resultField",
            "workflowIds"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "publicDocument",
        "openapi",
        "callerDirectory",
        "workflows"
      ],
      "description": "Paths only, no credential: present in every state."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "status",
    "seat",
    "authorization",
    "authorizedSurfaces",
    "discovery"
  ],
  "description": "CHAT §2.10: the room directory a room link carries, returned as `roomLinkUsage` by GET and POST /api/v1/chat/external-agent-invites/{inviteToken}."
}
```

## ConfirmationChallenge

```json
{
  "allOf": [
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AesErrorEnvelope"
    },
    {
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "confirmation"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "confirmation": {
          "type": "object",
          "required": [
            "confirmationId",
            "operationId",
            "expiresAt",
            "impact",
            "confirm"
          ],
          "properties": {
            "confirmationId": {
              "type": "string",
              "pattern": "^cfm_"
            },
            "operationId": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "expiresAt": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "date-time"
            },
            "impact": {
              "type": "object",
              "required": [
                "summary",
                "targets"
              ],
              "properties": {
                "summary": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "description": "Every consequence of the delete, naming the target by its label."
                },
                "targets": {
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "required": [
                      "kind",
                      "ref",
                      "label"
                    ],
                    "properties": {
                      "kind": {
                        "type": "string"
                      },
                      "ref": {
                        "type": "string"
                      },
                      "label": {
                        "type": "string"
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            },
            "confirm": {
              "type": "object",
              "required": [
                "method",
                "path",
                "headers",
                "body"
              ],
              "description": "The request to send to execute the delete.",
              "properties": {
                "method": {
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "path": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "description": "Path and query string."
                },
                "headers": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": [
                    "X-Starholder-Confirmation"
                  ],
                  "properties": {
                    "X-Starholder-Confirmation": {
                      "type": "string"
                    }
                  }
                },
                "body": {
                  "description": "The JSON body to resend, or null."
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/HuelvaContextPacketKind"
          }
        },
        "runtimePathRefs": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "proofRefs": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "gateRefs": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "observationPacketKinds",
        "runtimePathRefs",
        "proofRefs"
      ]
    },
    "interpretation": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "askRestatement": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "confidence": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "high",
            "medium",
            "low"
          ]
        },
        "worldRoleBindings": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/HuelvaWorldRoleBindings"
        },
        "catalogEntryRefs": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "chosenInterpretation": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "consideredAlternatives": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "interpretation": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "rejectedBecause": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "interpretation",
              "rejectedBecause"
            ]
          }
        },
        "bindingProvenance": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "inputName": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "derivedFrom": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "explicit_user_text",
                  "normalized_mention",
                  "directory_operand",
                  "catalog_fact",
                  "referent",
                  "prior_step_output"
                ]
              },
              "sourceRef": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "inputName",
              "derivedFrom"
            ]
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "askRestatement",
        "confidence",
        "worldRoleBindings",
        "catalogEntryRefs",
        "chosenInterpretation",
        "consideredAlternatives",
        "bindingProvenance"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "proposalId",
    "workstreamId",
    "createdAt",
    "proposalState",
    "surfaceKind",
    "motivatingReferent",
    "summary",
    "action",
    "authority",
    "approval",
    "expectedOutputs",
    "costEstimate",
    "evidence",
    "interpretation"
  ]
}
```

```json
              },
```

```json
                "type": "string"
              },
              "sourceOutputKey": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "sourceOutputSemanticTypeRef": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "onMissing": {
                "type": "string",
                "const": "omit"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "inputName",
              "sourceKind",
              "valueSummary"
            ]
          }
        },
        "capabilityContractPin": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "operationRef": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "ownerFamily": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "operationVersion": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "digestVersion": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "v2"
            },
            "contractDigest": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "inputSchemaDigest": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "resultSchemaDigest": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "idempotency": {

            "presentationDigest": {
              "type": "string"

            "snapshotDigest": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "callerAvailabilityDigest": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "operationRef",
            "ownerFamily",
            "operationVersion",
            "digestVersion",
            "contractDigest",
            "inputSchemaDigest",
            "resultSchemaDigest",
            "idempotency"
          ]
        },
        "groundingStatus": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "proven_satisfiable",
            "partially_grounded"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "executionClass",
        "targetingMode",
        "handoffOwner",
        "dispatchMode",
        "targetWorldIds",
        "workHomeWorldIds",
        "intentKinds",
        "inputBindings",
        "groundingStatus"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "targetRef",
    "actionDigest",
    "action"
  ]
}
```

## HuelvaLibraryRetrievalDriftFinding

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "capabilityRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "storedOperationRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "currentOperationRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "rootSource": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "semantic_root",
        "graph_neighbor",
        "standing_work"
      ]
    },
    "disposition": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "drop_missing_capability",
        "drop_operation_ref_mismatch"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "capabilityRef",
    "storedOperationRef",
    "rootSource",
    "disposition"
  ],
  "description": "Stale-pair finding raised before prompt admission sees a `capabilityRef`/`operationRef` pair whose stored `operationRef` no longer matches the row's current sealed identity, or whose `capabilityRef` no longer resolves to a current graph-admitted public row at all (`HUELVA_LIBRARY_OPERATOR_MIGRATION.md §4.8`). `lib/huelva/catalog/catalogService.ts` is the canonical owner of appending these to the retrieval trace (`HOPL-D-06`); `progressiveRetrieval.ts` preserves them and `lib/huelva/shell/prototype.ts` may log/render them diagnostically but never repairs a stale pair from Neo4j."
}
```

```json
                  "resultSchemaDigest": {

                  },

      },
```

```json
    },
    "driftFindings": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/HuelvaLibraryRetrievalDriftFinding"
      },
      "description": "Stale-pair findings carried through from this call's `HuelvaLibraryRetrievalTrace.driftFindings` (`HUELVA_LIBRARY_OPERATOR_MIGRATION.md §4.8`, `HOPL-D-07`): this is the \"prototype-facing types\" half of that ticket's drift-preservation requirement. `prototype.ts` only ever logs/renders these; it never repairs a stale pair."
    },
    "capabilityLaneFailureCode": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "lexicalRootCount": {
      "type": "number"
    },

      "type": "string"

      }

    "exactAnchorEntryIds",

  ]
}
```

## HuelvaReachedCapability

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "capabilityRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "operationRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "rootSource": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "semantic_root",
        "graph_neighbor",
        "standing_work"
      ]
    },
    "sourceEntryIds": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "graphDistance": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "semanticScore": {
      "type": "number"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "capabilityRef",
    "operationRef",
    "rootSource",
    "sourceEntryIds",
    "graphDistance"
  ],
  "description": "Capability reached by the single retrieval plane (HUELVA_REASONING_AMENDMENT §24.3): a direct semantic root over `operator_capability_embedding`, an `EXPOSES_CAPABILITY` graph neighbor of a materialized entry, or an identifier-keyed join from an inspected standing artifact whose validated tasks name the capability (revision provenance, §24.4).\n\n`operationRef` is the dual-identity floor from `HUELVA_LIBRARY_OPERATOR_MIGRATION.md §4.8` (`HOPL-C-01`). It is required, per `§4.8`: `lib/huelva/catalog/catalogService.ts` is the sole construction site (`HOPL-D-06`) and resolves it from the live `operationRef`-carrying Neo4j read (`OperatorCapabilityRootHit`/`OperatorCapabilityExposure`, `HOPL-D-05`) before validating it against the current sealed index; a capability whose current identity cannot be confirmed is dropped as a `HuelvaLibraryRetrievalDriftFinding`, never admitted without one. `lib/huelva/catalog/progressiveRetrieval.ts` and `lib/huelva/shell/prototype.ts` only preserve/read this field."
}
```

## HuelvaTextWorkbenchDraftRequest

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "userMessage": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "voicePolicyId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "editType": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "full_document"
    },
    "documentContent": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "userMessage",
    "voicePolicyId",
    "editType",
    "documentContent"
  ],
  "description": "Same-turn settlement of a staged artifact whose approval metadata carries no remaining planes (`approval.autoRunnable === true`, §13.7 mapping). The permission boundary (§25.7) places sub-mutation work below the confirmation obligation, so the shell must not stop at staging for it.\n\n- `client_follow`: the product effect is viewer navigation; the surface   executes the user's own command through its canonical focused-action   lane. `command` is the user's literal ask text — never manufactured.\n- `client_textworkbench`: the product effect is a local drafting handoff;   the surface opens TextWorkbench and starts the draft stream there.\n- `client_story_workspace`: the product effect is a local story-reader   handoff; the surface opens the canonical Story workspace for the exact   published TextRoot the staged row already bound, optionally carrying one   supporting passage anchor returned by story inference or passage-mode   story read.\n- `client_media_workspace`: the product effect is a local media-browser   handoff; the surface opens the canonical Media workspace for the exact   media search result set the owner already produced.\n- `client_make_prompt`: the product effect is a local Make / generator   handoff; the surface opens the generator and submits prompt-build there.\n- `client_make_execute`: the product effect is confirmation of the current   prompt-ready Make state; the surface submits the existing generate-execute   flow with that state rather than rebuilding the prompt.\n- `auto_run`: the shell already invoked the canonical approval   followthrough owner in this turn; `status` is that owner's verdict,   receipts included."
}
```

## PEEContextAnchorPreviewEvent

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "type": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "context_anchor_preview"
    },
    "context": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "contextText": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "semanticInputConcepts": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SourceConceptPhrase"
          },
          "description": "Canonical user-input phrase evidence, available from the first preview."
        },
        "primarySubject": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "ref": {
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "label": {
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "type": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "entity",
                    "setting"
                  ]
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "ref",
                "label",
                "type"
              ]
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "canonicalSetting": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/CanonicalSetting"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "atmosphere": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AtmosphereSignals"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "priorityTerms": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "visualSignature": {
          "type": [
            "string",
            "null"
          ]
        },
        "essence": {
          "type": [
            "string",
            "null"
          ]
        },
        "semanticSceneProfile": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SemanticSceneProfile"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "derivationMode": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DerivationMode"
        }
      }
    },
    "confidence": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "progressive": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/PEEProgressiveSurfacePatch"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "type",
    "context",
    "confidence",
    "progressive"
  ]
}
```

## PEEProgressiveSurfacePatch

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "authority": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/PEEProgressiveSurfaceAuthority"
    },
    "derivationMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "ref_acceptance",
        "media_bundle",
        "media_placement",
        "atmosphere_context",
        "thoughtpacket_commit",
        "context_anchor_preview",
        "retrieval_attention",
        "thoughtpacket_preview",
        "surface_complete",
        "client_session_fallback"
      ],
      "description": "Names the deterministic source pathway that produced this patch. This lets clients distinguish final explicit surface fields from preview material without inferring authority from event names."
    },
    "provisionalId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Stable id for provisional material that can later upgrade in place."
    },
    "source": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "authority",
    "derivationMode"
  ]
}
```

## PEERefAcceptedEvent

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "type": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "ref_accepted"
    },
    "ref": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "renderState": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "render_provisional",
        "commit_validated"
      ]
    },
    "progressive": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/PEEProgressiveSurfacePatch"
    },
    "label": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Optional canonical label for the ref. When provided, the client surface accumulator paints the dock tile / mention chip with this label during streaming instead of waiting for `surface_complete` to authoritatively fill it in. Spec: docs/design/CONSOLE_CENTER_DIV.md §1, §9.4 — progressive rendering during live turns."
    },
    "refType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "entity",
        "setting",
        "textroot",
        "packet"
      ],
      "description": "Optional ref type derived from the canonical ref prefix. Carried alongside `label` so the dock can pick the right typographic affordance during streaming. Mirrors  {@link  AcceptedRef.type  }  from the client surface."
    },
    "provenance": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Optional provenance string for diagnostic / lifecycle telemetry. Mirrors the `RefEdge.provenance` enum at the point of acceptance."
    },
    "href": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Optional canonical href (link target) for the ref. The classic console uses this to wire ref chip clicks; the new console honors the same payload for parity."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "type",
    "ref",
    "renderState"
  ]
}
```

```json

  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "submissionId": {

    },
    "status": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "pending"
    },

    },
    "blockingReason": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SubmissionBlockingReason"

          "type": "null"
        }
      ],
      "description": "Same as workflow.blockingReason."
    },
    "workflow": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SubmissionWorkflowView",
      "description": "The submission workflow view (ECA §6.3), as on every submission read."
    },
    "retryAfterMs": {
      "type": "number"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "submissionId",
    "status",
    "submissionStatus",
    "blockingReason",
    "workflow",
    "retryAfterMs"
  ],
  "description": "ECA §6.3 — a submission read answered while extraction is still running (HTTP 202 with Retry-After): GET .../manifest, and GET .../extraction before extraction has written output. What it reads does not exist yet; read again after retryAfterMs."
}
```

## SubmissionFinalizationLedger

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "schemaVersion": {
      "type": "number",
      "const": 1
    },
    "status": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/LedgerStatus"
    },
    "currentAttemptId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "finalizedAt": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "lastUpdatedAt": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "slotResults": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SlotResult"
      },
      "description": "Keyed by slotId (never by a legacy-only itemId).\n\nRetry rules (§8.4): 1. If phase >= 'imported', retry reuses importedAssetId/importedUrl. 2. If phase >= 'graph_written', retry skips the authoritative Cypher. 3. If phase >= 'linked', retry does not relink.\n\nConcurrency (§8.4):\n- Each attempt generates a new attemptId.\n- A concurrent request seeing status='running' + different attemptId +   lastUpdatedAt newer than 15min fails with ERR_FINALIZE_ALREADY_RUNNING.\n- Stale attempts older than 15min may be reclaimed."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "schemaVersion",
    "status",
    "lastUpdatedAt",
    "slotResults"
  ]
}
```

## SubmissionProductionLane

```json
{
  "type": "string",
  "enum": [
    "lazy_lane",
    "attended",
    "undecided"
  ],
  "description": "ECA §6.2 production lanes. `lazy_lane` is `platform_default`: the platform produces everything, pausing only for an extraction review the submitter requested. `attended` is `self_fulfill` / `post_bounty`: the platform produces extraction, graph, TTS, and the work manifest; media comes from the submitter or a bounty. `undecided` has no strategy yet. The lane can change until media production starts (ECA §6.2.3)."
}
```

## SubmissionWaitingOn

```json
{
  "type": "string",
  "enum": [
    "extraction",
    "platform",
    "creator",
    "bounty_fulfillers",
    "none"
  ],
  "description": "Who must act before the submission advances. `extraction`: the extraction pipeline is still running, so extraction, manifest, and fulfillment reads are not yet meaningful. `platform`: platform media production, assembly, or registration. `creator`: the submitter. `bounty_fulfillers`: a posted media bounty. `none`: terminal."
}
```

## SubmissionWorkflowAction

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "action": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Stable id: poll_submission, get_extraction, submit_correction, approve_extraction, set_brief, choose_strategy, get_manifest, request_upload_url, submit_media, review_media, get_fulfillment, review_bounty_submission, complete, get_playback."
    },
    "method": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "GET",
        "POST",
        "PUT"
      ]
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Absolute API path. Submission routes, except review_bounty_submission, which is the linked marketplace bounty's review route, and get_playback, which is the registered story's TextRoot read (`textroot_get`)."
    },
    "description": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "allowedStrategies": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/FulfillmentStrategy"
      },
      "description": "choose_strategy only: the strategies the submission can still take (ECA §6.2.3). The orchestrator refuses a selection once media production starts (ERR_STRATEGY_SELECTION_CLOSED)."
    },
    "body": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "required": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        },
        "optional": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentWorkflowBodyField"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "required",
        "optional"
      ],
      "description": "The request body, in the AES §8.10.7 workflow-step field form, where the path alone does not make the call (review_bounty_submission)."
    },
    "requires": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "action": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "field": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "in": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "action",
        "field",
        "in"
      ],
      "description": "The action is legal only once the read the named nextActions entry makes reports `field` in `in`; before that its route refuses it. Listed actions without `requires` are valid as soon as they are listed."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "action",
    "method",
    "path",
    "description"
  ]
}
```

## SubmissionWorkflowView

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "lane": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SubmissionProductionLane"
    },
    "strategy": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/FulfillmentStrategy"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ],
      "description": "fulfillmentStrategy ?? requestedWorkStrategy."
    },
    "strategySource": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "enum": [
        "selected",
        "requested",
        null
      ],
      "description": "`selected`: recorded as fulfillmentStrategy. `requested`: named at submit as requestedWorkStrategy; it is the decision, and the platform applies it when extraction completes (or at approval when reviewed) with no further call. `null`: undecided."
    },
    "extractionReview": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SubmissionExtractionReviewState"
    },
    "waitingOn": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SubmissionWaitingOn"
    },
    "blockingReason": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SubmissionBlockingReason"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "nextActions": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SubmissionWorkflowAction"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "lane",
    "strategy",
    "strategySource",
    "extractionReview",
    "waitingOn",
    "blockingReason",
    "nextActions"
  ],
  "description": "ECA §6.3 — the submission's workflow contract, derived from the record: the production lane, extraction-review state, who it waits on, the one reason it is blocked, and the calls valid now. Follow `nextActions`; an empty list means the submission is terminal."
}
```

```json
{
  "type": "object",
```

```json
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Title of the document"

      "type": "string",

    },
    "openMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "focus",
        "minimized"
      ],
      "description": "Optional open mode hint (default: minimized unless autoFocus/openMode=focus)"
    },
    "autoFocus": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "If true, open focus mode immediately"
    },
    "publishedTextRootId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "If provided, the minimized card should behave as a published story (open TRBV)"
    },
    "publishedCanonId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Canon id for TRBV when publishedTextRootId is provided"
    },
    "sourceAssetId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Optional linkage back to the source draft asset"
    },
    "creativeBrief": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Optional creative brief for workbench focus mode"
    },
    "sourceType": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/TextWorkbenchSourceType"
        },
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/TextWorkbenchOpenSourceType"
        }
      ]
    },
    "voicePolicyId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "provenance": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "conversationId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "messageIndex": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "messageId": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "id of the ChatMessageModel that was opened into TextWorkbench"
        },
        "turnId": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "HAM turnId of the source assistant message"
        },
        "bountyId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "claimId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "sourceGapId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "worldId": {
          "type": [
            "string",
            "null"
          ],
          "description": "worldId from the console surface at open time. Null when the flyout model lacks a worldId at dispatch time; the HAM open seam (TWB-C-01) records worldResolution:'missing_from_flyout_model' when this field is null."
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

## UserInboxSourceKind

```json
{
  "type": "string",
  "enum": [
    "first_contact_request",
    "room_invite",
    "world_invite"
  ],
  "description": "USER_INBOX Lane — Canonical Contract Types (C.USER_INBOX.*)\n\nSpec:   docs/HAM/build/USER_INBOX.md §2 Owner:  product / recipient inbox conformance File:   lib/ham/user-inbox/contracts/types.ts\n\nThis file is the single source of truth for every C.USER_INBOX.* contract shape. All USER_INBOX lane modules and all downstream consumers import from here.\n\nPure types only — no runtime values, no runtime imports.\n\nBuild order matches UIN-A-* ticket dependency chain:   A-02  C.USER_INBOX.source.materialization.v1   A-03  C.USER_INBOX.message.row.v1   A-04  C.USER_INBOX.state.cursor.v1   A-05  C.USER_INBOX.message.read.v1   A-06  C.USER_INBOX.console.view.v1   A-07  C.USER_INBOX.summary.response.v1   A-08  C.USER_INBOX.list.page.v1   A-09  C.USER_INBOX.open.mutation.v1   A-10  C.USER_INBOX.archive.mutation.v1\n\nBoundary:   - USER_INBOX owns the recipient-global normalized contracts below.   - CHAT and WORLDS remain the canonical source-truth owners and must not     redefine these recipient-facing shapes locally."
}
```

## WorldCommitReceiptV1

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "schemaVersion": {
      "type": "number",
      "const": 1
    },
    "executionId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "receiptId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "status": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ReceiptStatus"
    },
    "flowContext": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "flowId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "nodeId": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "description": "Intelligence Hub flow context (Phase 4+)."
    },
    "committedRevision": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Revision",
      "description": "Only present for non-pending receipts. Pending means convergence has not produced a committed revision yet."
    },
    "supersedesReceiptId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "When status === 'pending', a follow-up receipt may supersede this one. Receipts are immutable; progression is represented as a second receipt."
    },
    "ops": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "opId": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "status": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ReceiptOpStatus"
          },
          "durationMs": {
            "type": "number"
          },
          "error": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "opId",
          "status"
        ]
      }
    },
    "durableIdMap": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "failures": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "kind": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "count": {
            "type": "number"
          },
          "retryable": {
            "type": "boolean"
          },
          "details": {}
        },
        "required": [
          "kind",
          "retryable"
        ]
      }
    },
    "rebase": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "occurred": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "overlayBaseRevision": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Revision"
        },
        "rebasedOntoRevision": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Revision"
        },
        "resolutions": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "path": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "strategy": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "union",
                  "merge",
                  "lww"
                ]
              },
              "winner": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "overlay",
                  "committed"
                ]
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "path",
              "strategy"
            ]
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "occurred",
        "overlayBaseRevision",
        "rebasedOntoRevision",
        "resolutions"
      ],
      "description": "Rebase audit information (present when optimistic rebase occurred). Spec: docs/INTELLIGENCE_HUB.md §18.5.5"
    },
    "pointers": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "createdAt": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "schemaVersion",
    "executionId",
    "receiptId",
    "status",
    "ops",
    "createdAt"
  ]
}
```

## WorldConfigUpdateReceipt

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "worldId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "worldRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "configRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "priorConfigHash": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "newConfigHash": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "changedPaths": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "receiptKind": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "world.registry.updated"
    },
    "mirrorRefreshed": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "directoryDisposition": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "updated",
        "removed",
        "unchanged",
        "sync_failed"
      ],
      "description": "Outcome of the WorldDirectoryEntry sync that follows the config write.   updated     — entry was built and upserted into the public directory (world is now eligible)   removed     — entry was removed from the public directory (world no longer eligible)   unchanged   — store did not provide syncDirectoryEntry; sync was skipped for this call path   sync_failed — sync was attempted but failed (non-gating; repair required)"
    },
    "ham": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "status": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/WorldMutationHamStatus"
        },
        "turnId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "turnSnapshotRef": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "receiptRef": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "failureCode": {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/WorldMutationHamFailureCode"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "status"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "worldId",
    "configRef",
    "priorConfigHash",
    "newConfigHash",
    "changedPaths",
    "receiptKind",
    "mirrorRefreshed",
    "directoryDisposition",
    "ham"
  ]
}
```
